## Corpus Check
- 1538 files · ~2,242,785 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 54 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 16, .bat 5, .css 5)

## Summary
- 32683 nodes · 67203 edges · 1305 communities (1133 shown, 172 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 90% EXTRACTED · 10% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6616 edges (avg confidence: 0.93)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `74e2c96f`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
